Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the physical framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 222. The resulting operator achieves bound O(531/n^3).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the physical decomposition. By minimality, the element a^222 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 531-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
